### `RC Structure/Main.py` (cli)

Main.py designs a single structure on the grouped two-system route that the design batches use: perimeter special moment frames are the seismic force-resisting system and every other beam and column is a gravity member. Members are grouped by three-story band and plan location, and the groups of one system tie (special-frame or gravity, column or beam) in a band share one design. The slab acts compositely with the beams (T and L sections, slab reinforcement designed). The search starts from a seed and iterates candidate designs: a feasibility phase, then a reduction phase, with the Grade 80 retry and the core-layout ladder (perimeter + core ring, then core lines) when a phase finds nothing. The selected design is then built as the nonlinear IMK model and run through gravity and modal analysis.

- 563 lines; defines Heartbeat, _clock, _feet, geometry_name, build_case, population_notes, batch_seed, runner_arguments, new_output_dir, method_text, grade_policy_text, approximate_period ...; external: Generate_Parameterized_Dataset, run_grouped_batch.
- Uses:
  - `RC Structure/Design/Concrete_Grades.py`: imports inside a function Design.Concrete_Grades; uses families; calls families (1x)
  - `RC Structure/Design/Grouped_Runner.py`: imports inside a function Design.Grouped_Runner; uses run, build_parser, DEFAULT_GROUPING, GROUPINGS; calls run (2x), build_parser (1x)
  - `RC Structure/Model/Analysis_Profile.py`: imports inside a function Model.Analysis_Profile: PROFILES
  - `RC Structure/Model/Member_Groups.py`: imports inside a function Model.Member_Groups; uses default_system_policy, is_system_policy, SFRS_LAYOUT_PERIMETER, SFRS_LAYOUT_PERIMETER_CORE, SFRS_LAYOUT_PERIMETER_CORE_LINES, layout_of, is_banded_policy; calls default_system_policy (2x), is_system_policy (1x), layout_of (1x), is_banded_policy (1x)
